&lt;project_context>
# 项目: schema_semantic_constrained_ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-16980
- SSH 连接命令: ssh westd-16980  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
紧急任务：分析 SciERC Llama con 的 DR=0.09% violation 详情。

**背景**：统一 eval 显示 SciERC Llama con DR=0.09%（非零），需要知道具体是几个 violation、哪些 relation type，以决定 paper claim 是否需要修改。

**步骤**（westd-16980，ssh_read_only）：

### Step 1: 找到 Llama con SciERC w5 的 eval/violation 结果文件
```bash
# 统一 eval 输出
ls /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/unified_eval/*scierc*llama*con* 2>/dev/null
# 也查原始 violation 详情
find /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs -name "*scierc*llama*con*" | sort
find /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs -path "*w5*" -name "*llama*" | sort
```

### Step 2: 读取 eval JSON 中的 violation 详情
```bash
# 读取完整 eval 结果
cat /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs/unified_eval/scierc_llama_con.json 2>/dev/null
# 如果有 violation report
find /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs -name "*violation*" -path "*scierc*llama*con*" | head -5
```

### Step 3: 如果 eval JSON 不含 violation 详情，手动检查
```bash
# 读取 w5 Llama con SciERC 的 jsonl
# 找到文件
find /root/autodl-tmp/schema_outputs -name "*llama*scierc*con*" -name "*.jsonl" | head -5
```

然后用 violation_checker 跑详细报告（如果有 --verbose 或 --detail 选项）：
```bash
cd /root/autodl-tmp/schema_semantic_constrained_ie
source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base

# 找 violation checker
find . -name "violation*" -name "*.py" | head -5

# 跑 detailed violation check（ssh_execute）
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES="" python [violation_checker] \
  --input [llama_con_scierc.jsonl] \
  --dataset scierc \
  --verbose \
  [其他参数]
```

### Step 4: 需要确认的信息
1. DR=0.09% 是几个 violation（绝对数量 / 总 triple 数）
2. 涉及哪些 relation type
3. 这些 violation 的具体内容（head entity type, relation, tail entity type）
4. 是否是 constraint 未覆盖的边缘情况

**输出**：
```
DR violation 数量: X / Y 总 triples (0.09%)
涉及 relation types: [列表]
具体 violation 示例: [head_type] --[relation]--> [tail_type] (expected: [correct_types])
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# WARNING: This checker is for SciERC only.\\n# For DocRED evaluation, use violation_checker_docred.py instead.\\n# Using this checker on DocRED outputs will produce false RV=100%\\n# because it iterates P-codes as relation names, not natural language labels.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Check ontology constraint violations in SciERC extraction outputs.\\n\\nViolation types (mutually exclusive per triple):\\n1. entity_type_violations      - entity type not in ontology's valid type list\\n2. relation_vocabulary_violations - relation name not in ontology (solvable by grammar-constrained decoding)\\n3. domain_range_violations     - relation name valid but (head_type, tail_type) violates constraints (OntoCoDec core target)\\n4. cross_field_consistency_violations - same entity mention has inconsistent types across relations\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_type(t, valid_types, type_map=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Case-insensitive type matching with common aliases.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if type_map is None:\\n        type_map = {\\n            \\\"otherscientificterm\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"other-scientificterm\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"other_scientific_term\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"scientificterm\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"other\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n        }\\n    t_lower = t.strip().lower().replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"_\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"-\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    for vt in valid_types:\\n        if t_lower == vt.lower().replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"_\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"-\\\", \\\"\\\"):\\n            return vt\\n    if t_lower in type_map:\\n        return type_map[t_lower]\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_relation(rel_name, valid_relation_types):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fuzzy-match a model-output relation name to the nearest ontology relation.\\n\\n    Match conditions (any one suffices):\\n    1. Exact match (case-insensitive, ignoring spaces/hyphens/underscores)\\n    2. Token overlap > 80%\\n    3. Levenshtein distance <= 2\\n\\n    Returns: (matched_name, is_fuzzy_match) or (None, False)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    rel_norm = rel_name.strip().lower().replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"_\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"-\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    for vrt in valid_relation_types:\\n        if rel_norm == vrt.lower().replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"_\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"-\\\", \\\"\\\"):\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.53}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# WARNING: This checker is for SciERC only.\\n# For DocRED evaluation, use violation_checker_docred.py instead.\\n# Using this checker on DocRED outputs will produce false RV=100%\\n# because it iterates P-codes as relation names, not natural language labels.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Check ontology constraint violations in SciERC extraction outputs.\\n\\nViolation types (mutually exclusive per triple):\\n1. entity_type_violations      - entity type not in ontology's valid type list\\n2. relation_vocabulary_violations - relation name not in ontology (solvable by grammar-constrained decoding)\\n3. domain_range_violations     - relation name valid but (head_type, tail_type) violates constraints (OntoCoDec core target)\\n4. cross_field_consistency_violations - same entity mention has inconsistent types across relations\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_type(t, valid_types, type_map=None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Case-insensitive type matching with common aliases.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if type_map is None:\\n        type_map = {\\n            \\\"otherscientificterm\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"other-scientificterm\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"other_scientific_term\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"scientificterm\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n            \\\"other\\\": \\\"OtherScientificTerm\\\",\\n        }\\n    t_lower = t.strip().lower().replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"_\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"-\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    for vt in valid_types:\\n        if t_lower == vt.lower().replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"_\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\"-\\\", \\\"\\\"):\\n            return vt\\n    if t_lower in type_map:\\n        return type_map[t_lower]\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_relation(rel_name, valid_relation_types):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fuzzy-match a model-output relation name to the nearest ontology relation.\\n\\n    Match conditions (any one suffices):\\n    1. ## 分析结果

### DR violation 数量
**1 / 1081** predicted triples (0.09%)

### 唯一的 violation 详情

| 字段 | 值 |
|------|-----|
| doc_id | `NIPS_2001_11_abs_sent4` |
| head | "CID/DRAM analog array prototype" |
| head_type | **OtherScientificTerm** |
| relation | **Evaluate-for** |
| tail | "approach" |
| tail_type | **Generic** |
| 违反原因 | `(OtherScientificTerm, Generic)` 不在 Evaluate-for 的 valid_pairs 中 |

### Evaluate-for 的合法 pairs（23 个）
Evaluate-for 的 valid_pairs 包含 `(OtherScientificTerm, Method)` 和 `(Generic, OtherScientificTerm)` 等 23 种组合，但**确实不包含 `(OtherScientificTerm, Generic)`**。

### 这是 constraint 未覆盖的边缘情况吗？

**是。** 训练集中 Evaluate-for 共 313 例，从未出现 `(OtherScientificTerm, Generic)` 这个 pair（分布中没有该键）。所以 schema 约束从训练数据统计得来，合理地不包含此 pair。模型在 constrained decoding 下仍输出了它，说明这 1 个 triple 是约束漏网的极罕见边缘情况。

### Paper claim 影响

DR=0.09% (1/1081) 对 paper claim 影响极小：
- 如果 paper 写的是 "DR≈0%" 或 "near-zero DR"——**不需要修改**，0.09% 可以 round 到 ≈0%
- 如果 paper 写的是 "DR=0%"（精确零）——需要改为 "0.09%" 或 "1 violation out of 1081 triples"
- 建议在表格中如实报告 0.09%，脚注说明这 1 个 violation 的来源是 `(OtherScientificTerm, Generic)` 在 Evaluate-for 中未被训练集覆盖
